### [88] [TCS-BENCH: Benchmarking State-of-the-Art Generative AI Theoretical Computer Science Research Ability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09538)
*Vincent Cohen-Addad,Dimitris Paparas,Ernest van Wijland,Max Springer,Julien Canitrot-Paradis,Honghao Lin,David Woodruff,Adarsh Kumarappan,Rajesh Jayaram,Rudrajit Das,Lalit Jain,Ola Svensson,Silvio Lattanzi,Mislav Balunovic,Theophane Weber,Vahab Mirrokni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍TCS-Bench，一个用于评估大语言模型在理论计算机科学（TCS）研究级定理证明能力的基准。该基准包含来自STOC、FOCS和SODA等顶级理论计算机科学会议论文中的定理证明任务，通过验证代理检查生成证明的正确性，并用人机对比验证验证器的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM评估基准多集中于一般数学问题或简单推理，缺乏对研究级理论计算机科学定理证明任务的系统性评测。理论计算机科学中的定理证明具有高度专业性和复杂性，需要一个专门的基准来评估LLM在该领域的推理和证明生成能力。

Method: 构建TCS-Bench基准，从STOC、FOCS、SODA三个顶级TCS会议论文中提取定理证明任务，为每个任务提供足够的上下文以便生成自包含证明。使用验证代理自动检查证明正确性，并将验证代理与人类专家对目标陈述和生成证明对的判断进行对比评测。

Result: 参考验证代理在专家标注的证明对数据集上达到了超过90%的准确率，验证了自动验证机制的有效性。同时基准评测了多种当前最先进的LLM在TCS定理证明任务上的表现。

Conclusion: TCS-Bench提供了一个可靠的自动化基准来评估LLM在理论计算机科学研究级定理证明方面的能力，其验证代理的准确性已通过人类专家标注的验证。

Abstract: We introduce TCS-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating Large Language Models (LLMs) on research-level Theoretical Computer Science (TCS) proof generation. TCS-Bench consists of theorem-proving tasks from papers published at top theoretical computer science venues (STOC, FOCS, and SODA). Each task provides the necessary context to derive a self-contained proof for a target result. We evaluate state-of-the-art models on this benchmark. We verify the correctness of generated proofs via a verification agent, and further benchmark the verifier against human-expert proof judgements on a set of target statements and generated proofs pairs. Our reference verifier achieves over 90% accuracy on the expert labeled set.

</details>


### [89] [Mawqif-v2: An Arabic Benchmark Dataset for Cross-Target Stance Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09539)
*Rasha Albalawi,Nuha Albadi,Hamzah Luqman,Maram Kurdi,Saad Ezzini,Asma Yamani,Ahmed Ashraf*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了Mawqif-v2扩展数据集，包含996条人工标注的阿拉伯推文，涉及三个公开目标（女性驾驶、电动汽车、学期制），每条推文均标注了立场、情感和讽刺标签。该扩展集作为留出评估集，用于评估模型在语义相关和全新未见过目标上的跨目标泛化能力。作者还建立了多种阿拉伯语和跨语言Transformer模型以及零样本大语言模型的基线结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公开可用的阿拉伯语目标特定立场检测数据集非常有限，尤其缺乏用于评估跨目标泛化能力的数据集，这阻碍了该领域的研究进展。

Method: 构建Mawqif-v2扩展数据集：从三个公开目标（女性驾驶、电动汽车、学期制）收集并人工标注996条阿拉伯推文，遵循原始Mawqif标注方案，每条推文包含立场、情感和讽刺三类标签。使用原始Mawqif数据集进行训练和开发，扩展集作为held-out评估集。评估使用多种阿拉伯语/跨语言Transformer模型和零样本LLM建立基线。

Result: 建立了多模型基线结果，涵盖阿拉伯语和跨语言Transformer模型以及零样本大语言模型，为后续可复现的跨目标泛化评估提供了参考基准。

Conclusion: Mawqif-v2扩展与原始Mawqif数据集共同构成一个用于评估阿拉伯语立场检测跨目标泛化能力的基准，填补了该领域公开评估资源的空白。

Abstract: Publicly available Arabic datasets for target-specific stance detection remain limited, particularly for evaluating cross-target generalization. This paper presents the Mawqif-v2 Extension, consisting of 996 manually annotated Arabic tweets collected from three public targets: Women Driving, E-Cars, and Trimester System. Each tweet is annotated with stance, sentiment, and sarcasm labels following the original Mawqif annotation scheme. The released extension is intended as a held-out evaluation set for assessing model generalization to both semantically related and previously unseen targets, while the original Mawqif dataset is used for training and development. In addition, we establish baseline results using several Arabic and multilingual transformer models, as well as zero-shot large language models (LLMs), to facilitate reproducible evaluation. Together with the original Mawqif dataset, the Mawqif-v2 Extension provides a benchmark for evaluating cross-target generalization in Arabic stance detection.

</details>


### [90] [ELBench: A Multi-Dimensional Benchmark for Education-Facing Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09548)
*Yilin Jiang,Xiaorong Zhu,Fei Tan,Zicheng Zhang,Kaiyi Huang,Yang Yu,Zexuan Fei,Yiming Luo,Keqian Li,Hao Hao,Aimin Zhou,Guangtao Zhai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ELBench，这是首个在同一协议下综合评估大语言模型教育适用性的基准，涵盖通用能力、安全与可信度、基础教育、高阶培养四大模块。基于对九个大模型（七个前沿通用模型和两个教育专用模型）的评估，发现模块级画像比单一总分更有信息量、国产模型在安全模块领先、教育专用模型未能在教育模块中领先，且所有模型在高阶培养的结构化判断任务上存在系统性盲区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在教育场景中承担导师、助教、内容生成等角色，需要同时满足准确性、安全性、教学实用性和教学目标匹配度等多重要求。然而现有基准大多孤立评估这些维度，缺少一个能在同一协议下综合评估教育适用性的整体画像式基准。

Method: 构建ELBench基准，在统一协议下对同一批模型评测四大模块：General Capability、Safety and Trustworthiness、Basic Education、High-Level Cultivation。数据来源结合精选公开语料与新增合成的安全和培育数据。评测九个模型，包括七个前沿通用系统与两个教育专用变体。

Result: 三项主要发现：(1) 模块级画像比单一总分更具区分度，前六名模型总分统计上无显著差异但模块领导者各不相同，安全与实操教学显著负相关（r=-0.83）；(2) 国产模型在最具区分度的安全模块领先，优势在区域特定规范内容上最大，在普遍性危害内容上缩小但未消失；(3) 两个教育专用模型未在两个教育模块中领先，且所有模型在高阶培养的结构化判断任务上存在系统性盲区——倾向于教学风格而非目标匹配度，模块分数普遍偏低且无法区分模型。

Conclusion: ELBench揭示了教育场景评测应关注多维度的模块画像而非单一总分。当前前沿模型在教育适用性上存在显著短板，特别是安全与教学能力间的权衡、教育专用模型的实际优势尚不明确、高阶培养能力（目标匹配判断）存在系统性缺陷。这些发现对教育AI的后续研究与实践部署具有重要启示。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed in education as tutors, teaching assistants, and content generators. These roles place demands that ordinary question answering does not: a usable education-facing model is supposed to be accurate, safe under sensitive prompts, instructionally useful, and aligned with pedagogical goals at the same time. Existing benchmarks evaluate these requirements largely in isolation, so none assesses education-facing suitability as an integrated profile. We introduce ELBench, the first benchmark to evaluate all four requirements (General Capability, Safety and Trustworthiness, Basic Education, and High-Level Cultivation) on the same models under a common protocol, combining curated public sources with newly synthesized safety and cultivation data. We evaluate nine models, seven frontier general-purpose systems and two education-specialized variants, and report three findings. First, module-level profiles are more informative than a single aggregate: the top six models are statistically indistinguishable on overall score, yet their module leaders differ substantially, and safety is anti-correlated with practical teaching (r = -0.83). Second, the Chinese-developed models lead the safety module, the most discriminative in the suite; this advantage is largest on region-specific normative content and narrows, but does not vanish, on universal-harm content. Third, the two education-specialized models lead neither education module, and on High-Level Cultivation all models share a systematic blind spot: on the structured judgment task they converge on the same non-reference option, favoring pedagogical style over fit to the stated goal, so the module scores uniformly low and does not separate models. This raises, but does not resolve, whether domain post-training keeps pace with frontier systems on education tasks.

</details>


### [91] [Pragmatic Attack Surface: Vulnerabilities of Implicit Context in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09551)
*Bocheng Chen,Han Zi,Roucheng Ou,Yawei Liu,Minyue Chen,Zimo Qi,Rongrong Wang,Guangliang Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"语用攻击面"（pragmatic attack surface），指出大语言模型的安全对齐（safety alignment）未能充分利用人类语言中隐含的语用语境（如世界知识、社会规范），攻击者可利用这一不匹配绕过LLM的安全机制，实验证明该方法在多个开源和闭源模型上显著优于基线攻击方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全对齐算法主要针对显式语言线索的劫持攻击（如直接诱导不安全输出），但这些攻击往往能被已有的安全对齐缓解。然而，人类语言本质上依赖于语用学，需要借助隐含的典型语境（如世界知识、社会规范）来正确理解语言，而这些语境并未在安全对齐中被充分利用，导致安全对齐与人类语言理解之间存在根本性不匹配，暴露了新的脆弱性。

Method: 本文识别并形式化定义了"语用攻击面"这一新的脆弱性类别，即通过利用人类语言中隐含的语用语境（而非显式恶意语言线索）来构造攻击提示，从而绕过LLM的安全机制，实现高攻击成功率。

Result: 实验结果显示，所提出的语用攻击方法在多种开源和闭源LLM上均以较大差距超越了基线攻击方法，取得了更高的攻击成功率。

Conclusion: LLM安全对齐与人类语用语言理解之间存在根本性不匹配，这种不匹配构成了可被利用的攻击面。该发现揭示了当前安全对齐范式的不足，提示未来的安全对齐工作需要考虑人类语言的语用层面，将隐含语境纳入对齐训练中。

Abstract: In the era of large language models (LLMs), attackers often manipulate natural language to elicit unsafe or harmful outputs, creating a new natural language attack surface unique to LLM-based systems, where attacks directly exploit explicit linguistic cues in user prompts to bypass the safety mechanism of LLMs. However, such attacks can often be mitigated by existing safety alignment algorithms. On the other hand, human language is inherently grounded in pragmatics, necessitating typical context to interpret language, e.g., world knowledge, social norms. However, such contexts are often implicit because they are not directly expressed in human language and are not sufficiently leveraged in safety alignment, creating a fundamental mismatch between human language interpretation and safety alignment approaches. In this paper, we demonstrate that this mismatch exposes vulnerabilities in LLMs. We refer to this vulnerability as the pragmatic attack surface, which can be exploited to achieve high attack success rates. The experimental results demonstrate that our proposed approach outperforms baseline attack methods across various open-source and closed-source models by a substantial margin.

</details>


### [92] [Se-DPO: Self-Evolving Token Credit for Direct Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09568)
*Wenxiao Zhao,Shu Wang,Ying Nian Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Se-DPO（Self-Evolving Token Credit for DPO）是一种改进直接偏好优化（DPO）的方法。传统DPO通过均匀求和聚合token级别的对数概率比，隐含假设所有token对偏好信号的贡献相同。Se-DPO引入"token credit"概念，根据每个token对偏好结果的贡献动态调节其KL正则化强度，并从模型自身演化的内部信号中实时推导token credit，同时基于每个token贡献的强度与置信度进行校准，且无需外部模型，仅增加一个轻量校准网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统DPO通过均匀求和聚合token级对数概率比，隐含地将所有token视为对偏好信号等权重贡献。但实际上不同token对偏好信号的贡献存在显著差异。作者推导出有效的token credit正比于每个token隐式奖励的幅度，并发现该量在训练过程中会发生显著变化，这意味着静态的token credit会随着训练推进而越来越不匹配实际情况。

Method: 提出Se-DPO：1）引入token credit概念，根据每个token对偏好结果的贡献调节其KL正则化强度；2）推导出token credit与隐式奖励幅度成正比；3）由于奖励信号在不同位置的可信度不同，Se-DPO基于每个token贡献的强度与置信度两者来校准token credit；4）从模型自身在DPO训练中演化的内部信号出发，实时（live）推导token credit；5）无需外部模型，仅引入一个轻量校准网络，计算开销极小。

Result: 实验表明Se-DPO相比标准DPO在AlpacaEval 2上最高提升9.8个百分点，在Arena-Hard上最高提升12.2个百分点。

Conclusion: Se-DPO通过动态、自演化的token级credit分配，有效解决了传统DPO中静态等权token处理的缺陷，在极小计算开销下显著提升了偏好优化性能，验证了基于token贡献度动态调节正则化强度的有效性。

Abstract: Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) aggregates token-level log-probability ratios via uniform summation, implicitly treating all tokens as contributing equally to the preference signal. However, the contribution of individual tokens to the preference signal varies. We introduce token credit, which modulates each token's KL regularization based on its contribution to the preference outcome. We derive that effective token credit is proportional to the magnitude of each token's implicit reward, and observe that this quantity evolves substantially during training. This implies that static token credit becomes increasingly misaligned as training progresses. In this work, we propose Se-DPO (Self-Evolving Token Credit for DPO), a live mechanism that derives token credit from the model's own evolving internal signals during DPO training. Since the reward signal varies in reliability across positions, Se-DPO calibrates token credit based on both the strength and the confidence of each token's contribution. Se-DPO requires no external models, adding only a lightweight calibration network with minimal computational overhead. Experiments show that Se-DPO improves over DPO by up to 9.8 points on AlpacaEval~2 and 12.2 points on Arena-Hard.

</details>


### [93] [MDB-Link: Hierarchical Schema Linking for Multi-Database Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09588)
*Beiyu Xu,Zhenyu Wu,Jiaoyan Chen,Riza theresa Batista-navarro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MDB-Link，一个面向多数据库集合的层级式schema linking框架。该框架先通过全局索引检索相关列，再聚合证据缩小数据库候选范围，最后用预算感知的LLM进行数据库重排、表选择和列定位，在MMQA、Spider2-Snow和BIRD-dev三个基准上显著超越现有方法（如LinkAlign），同时保持更快的运行速度和接近gold schema的紧凑schema子集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Text-to-SQL研究和基准假设目标数据库已知，忽略了查询需要在大量异构数据库集合中进行路由的实际场景。在多数据库环境中，系统首先需要定位目标数据库，然后构建紧凑的、与SQL生成相关的schema，这一挑战尚未被充分研究。

Method: MDB-Link采用层级式schema linking框架：1）从全局索引中检索与问题相关的列；2）聚合检索证据以缩小数据库候选范围；3）使用预算感知的LLM进行数据库重排、表选择和列定位。整个流程通过分层缩减逐步聚焦，最终生成紧凑的SQL相关schema子集用于下游SQL生成。

Result: 使用Qwen2.5-14B时，MDB-Link在数据库定位和列选择上全面超越LinkAlign。Exact match从16.88提升至51.41（MMQA）、从2.50提升至9.17（Spider2-Snow）、从12.52提升至38.01（BIRD-dev）。同时，MDB-Link生成的schema子集大小接近gold schema，且运行速度快于LinkAlign和AutoLink。

Conclusion: MDB-Link验证了层级式schema缩减在多数据库Text-to-SQL场景中的有效性，既能准确定位目标数据库和关键列，又能保持紧凑的schema子集以提升下游SQL生成的效率和准确性。

Abstract: Traditional Text-to-SQL research and benchmarks assume a known target database, overlooking settings in which a query must be routed within a large, heterogeneous database collection. We therefore study schema linking in a multi-database setting, where the system must first locate the target database and then construct a compact, SQL-relevant schema for generation. We propose MDB-Link, a hierarchical schema-linking framework that retrieves question-relevant columns from a global index, aggregates retrieval evidence to shortlist databases, and uses a budget-aware large language model (LLM) for database reranking, table selection, and column grounding. With Qwen2.5-14B, MDB-Link outperforms LinkAlign on MMQA, Spider2-Snow, and BIRD-dev in database localization and column selection while producing schema subsets close in size to the gold schemas. Exact match improves from 16.88 to 51.41 on MMQA, 2.50 to 9.17 on Spider2-Snow, and 12.52 to 38.01 on BIRD-dev. MDB-Link also runs faster than LinkAlign and AutoLink, demonstrating the effectiveness of hierarchical schema reduction for downstream SQL generation.

</details>


### [94] [How Do Large Language Models Judge Social Attraction? Evidence from Theory-Grounded Persona Ratings Across Multiple LLMs and Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09717)
*Hasan Mahmud,Khawaja Abaid Ullah,Mohammad Javad Khojasteh,Jamison Heard,Prabu David*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过三项研究检验LLM作为社会评价者的有效性，发现LLM在评估理论驱动的社交吸引力人格画像时表现出良好的跨运行稳定性、三层级排序一致性以及与人类评价者在相对排序上的一致性，但LLM在极端画像上的评分更趋向极端（吸引者更高、不吸引者更低），且两者均未表现出显著的性别呈现效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地被用于执行传统上由人类完成的主观评价任务，但其作为社会判断者的效度尚不明确。本文旨在检验LLM能否基于理论构建的人格画像评估社会吸引力，并系统比较LLM与人类的评价行为。

Method: 研究采用三个实验设计：研究1让34个LLM对12个基于十个心理与人际关系构念构建的三层级（社交吸引/混合/不吸引）画像进行三轮重复评分；研究2通过性别匹配的名称和代词配对测试LLM对性别呈现的敏感性；研究3招募198名人类参与者对研究2中的六对画像进行评价，与LLM结果进行对比。

Result: LLM在跨运行中表现出强稳定性、一致的三层级排序和高度的相对排序一致性，尽管不同模型在总体评分倾向上存在差异。性别呈现对LLM和人类均无显著影响。LLM与人类的相对画像排序一致，但LLM在吸引与不吸引画像上的评分比人类更极端。

Conclusion: LLM在评估社交吸引力画像时具有相当的效度，能复现人类的三层级结构与排序逻辑，但存在评分极化倾向。性别呈现对LLM和人类判断均无显著影响，表明LLM在此维度上未表现出明显的性别偏差。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to perform subjective evaluations traditionally made by humans, yet their validity as social judges remains unclear. This paper examines whether LLMs can assess social attraction from theory-grounded persona profiles constructed from ten psychological and relational constructs and organized into three tiers: socially attractive, socially mixed, and socially unattractive. We examine LLM ratings in two studies and compare them with human judgments in a third study. In Study 1, 34 LLMs rated 12 profiles across three repeated runs. Although some models tended to give higher or lower ratings overall, they showed strong stability across runs, consistent three-tier ordering, and high agreement in relative profile ordering. Study 2 examined sensitivity to gender presentation using six matched name-and-pronoun profile pairs and a separate pronoun-only test with a gender-neutral name, finding no significant effects in either analysis. In Study 3, 198 human participants evaluated the six matched profiles from Study 2. Their ratings reproduced the three-tier structure and followed a profile ordering consistent with that of the LLMs. However, LLMs rated attractive profiles more positively and unattractive profiles more negatively than humans, while neither group showed a significant overall effect of gender presentation.

</details>


### [95] [REFRAMED: Towards Realistic Audio Description Generation for Movies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09765)
*Igor Sterner,Mirella Lapata,Alex Lascarides,Frank Keller*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的音频描述（AD）生成任务表述，要求模型同时决定"描述什么"和"何时描述"，并发布了REFRAMED高质量数据集（2,023个视频、3,302个场景、206部电影），包含专业AD转录、字幕和对齐剧本，以及人工策划的挑战集和评估协议。实验表明现有最先进系统远超平凡基线但仍远不及人类专家水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AD生成方法在人为设定的人工环境中进行，即描述内容和时间点都已预先指定，将任务降级为片段级字幕生成。此外，现有方法依赖有噪声的转录和对齐流程，缺乏建模叙事上下文所需的丰富平行数据。作者认为AD本质上是一项结构化的编辑任务，描述必须插入对话间隙且只传达理解叙事所需的信息，因此需要全新的任务表述和数据集。

Method: 作者重新定义了AD生成任务，要求模型联合决策描述内容和描述时机。为此构建了REFRAMED数据集，包含来自206部电影的2,023个视频、3,302个场景，提供美版和英版专业AD转录、专业字幕和对齐的剧本。同时构建了人工策划的挑战集，将完整电影与多个AD参考配对，并提出了利用对话间隙和多参考比较的评估协议。

Result: 对最先进的AD系统和多模态大语言模型进行实验评估，结果表明这些模型虽然优于平凡基线，但距离人类专家性能仍有显著差距，凸显了该任务的挑战性和现有方法的不足。

Conclusion: REFRAMED数据集和基准测试为视频理解研究建立了新的基础，该工作将AD生成从简化的片段级字幕生成推进到需要叙事理解和时序决策的完整任务，为该领域未来研究提供重要资源。

Abstract: Audio Description (AD) is a verbal narration of key visual content in videos, enabling access for visually impaired audiences. Unlike standard video captioning, AD is a structured editorial task: descriptions must be inserted into gaps in dialogue and must convey only what is needed to understand the narrative being told. However, existing approaches formulate AD generation in an artificial setting where both the content and timing of descriptions are pre-specified, reducing the task to clip-level captioning. They further rely on noisy transcription and alignment pipelines, and lack the rich parallel data required for modeling narrative context. We introduce a new formulation of AD generation in which models must jointly decide what to describe and when to do it. To support this, we present REFRAMED, a high-quality dataset of 2,023 videos that span 3,302 scenes from 206 movies, with professional AD transcripts (both American and British versions), professional subtitles and aligned screenplays. We also provide a manually curated challenge set that pairs full movies with multiple AD references, together with evaluation protocols that leverage dialogue gaps and multi-reference comparisons. Experiments with state-of-the-art AD systems and multimodal LLMs show that they outperform trivial baselines but fall far short of expert human performance. Our dataset and benchmark establish a new foundation for research on video understanding.

</details>


### [96] [Cultivar: A Contrastive and Locale-Oriented Translation Benchmark for Investigating Contamination and Localisation Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09766)
*Pinzhen Chen,Koel Dutta Chowdhury,Xiaoya Xu,David Tan,Doreen Osmelak,Ona de Gibert,Ariun-Erdene Tumurchuluun,Ashok Urlana,Fedor Sizov,Hale Sirin,Jesujoba Alabi,Karrar Talib Abed,Mateusz Klimaszewski,Nikolay Bogoychev,Niyati Bafna,Patricia Schmidtova,Preksha Manjunath Shanbhag,Sherrie Shen,Vilem Zouhar,Vivek Iyer,Yasser Hamidullah,Yusser Al Ghussin,Zheng Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出源对比评估（source-contrastive evaluation）方法，并构建了Cultivar数据集——FLORES的本地化子集，用于评估机器翻译模型在不同地区与文化情境下的翻译表现，同时对32个开源模型进行了基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多语言翻译基准通常以英语为源头并翻译成其他语言，将语言对作为评估单元，这种设计容易随时间产生数据污染，且忽视了地域（locale）和文化差异。作者因此主张采用源对比评估，以更好地反映真实翻译场景。

Method: 作者提出源对比评估范式，并构建了Cultivar数据集（FLORES的本地化子集）。该方法将本地化内容与其未本地化的对应内容配对，通过两者之间的性能差异来探测数据污染和本地化鲁棒性。作者对32个开源权重模型进行了基准测试。

Result: 结果显示：机器翻译专用模型的鲁棒性较差；少数模型可能对FLORES数据集存在过拟合；无论使用何种语言，模型在翻译美国内容方面普遍优于其他地区的内容。

Conclusion: 研究表明现有翻译评估方式缺乏地域和文化维度，源对比评估与Cultivar数据集能更有效地暴露模型在数据污染和本地化适应方面的不足，为多语言翻译评估提供了新范式。

Abstract: Multilingual translation benchmarks are typically sourced in English and translated into other languages, treating language pairs as the unit of evaluation---a design that is prone to contamination over time and overlooks locale and cultural considerations. We therefore advocate for source-contrastive evaluation and instantiate it with Cultivar, a localised subset of FLORES, which enables locale-specific translation evaluation. When paired with unlocalised counterparts, performance discrepancy allows the probing of data contamination and localisation robustness. We benchmark 32 open-weight models and find that MT-specialised models are less robust, a few models potentially overfit FLORES, and models tend to translate US content better than that of other locales, regardless of language.

</details>


### [97] [Structured Phonological Representations for Audio-Articulatory rtMRI Speech Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09767)
*Abner Hernandez,Tomás Arias Vergara,Daiqi Liu,Andreas Maier,Paula Andrea Pérez-Toro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究PhonoQ（一种基于音频、以结构化音系特征监督训练的模型）是否能为音频-发音关节建模提供有用信息。通过在Conformer模块中提取表示并与WavLM-large和HuBERT-large基线对比，PhonoQ特征在未见语音和未见受试者场景下均提升了音系特征（方式、部位、嗓音、元音高度、元音前后性）的宏F1分数及39音素细粒度分类性能。此外，仅从发音轮廓推断音频时，来自音频的教师监督带来小幅但一致的性能提升，表明音系信息可部分从同步音频迁移至发音模型。后验分析还揭示了可解释的表层敏感模式，与/t/闪音化、/t/-/r/后缩或塞擦化、鼻音部位同化等语音学现象一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时MRI能够观测言语产生过程中的声道发音运动，但如何将这些发音运动模式映射到音系/语音学类别仍具挑战性。作者探索利用PhonoQ这一基于音频信号、监督信号为结构化音系特征的预训练模型，来增强音频-发音关节建模，以期提升音系类别预测能力。

Method: 从PhonoQ的Conformer模块提取表示（其训练受方式、部位、嗓音和元音特征的监督），将发音轮廓与同步音频特征结合，构建融合PhonoQ表示的模型，并与WavLM-large和HuBERT-large基线对比，在未见语音和未见受试者两种设置下评估。同时设计仅发音轮廓推理场景，测试音频教师监督是否能迁移到发音模型。

Result: PhonoQ特征在未见语音和未见受试者场景下均显著提升音系特征的宏F1（包括方式、部位、嗓音、元音高度和元音前后性），并改善了39音素的细粒度分类。在仅发音轮廓推理中，音频教师监督带来小幅但一致的增益。后验分析显示与闪音化/t/、/t/-/r/后缩或塞擦化、鼻音部位同化等一致的表面敏感模式。

Conclusion: PhonoQ的音系感知表示能有效增强音频-发音关节建模，证明结构化音系监督可以迁移到发音领域，并产生可解释的、与真实语音现象一致的发音-音系映射模式。

Abstract: Real-time MRI makes it possible to observe vocal-tract articulation during speech, but mapping these articulatory patterns to phonetic and phonological categories remains challenging. We investigate whether PhonoQ, an audio-based model trained to recognize structured phonological features, provides useful information for audio--articulatory modeling. Specifically, we extract representations from PhonoQ's Conformer module, whose training is shaped by supervision for manner, place, voicing, and vowel features. Using articulatory contours with synchronized audio-derived features, we compare WavLM-large and HuBERT-large baselines with models that incorporate PhonoQ-derived representations. Across unseen-speech and unseen-subject settings, these features improve macro-F1 for phonological targets including manner, place, voicing, vowel height, and vowel backness, and also improve fine-grained 39-phoneme classification. In a contour-only inference setting, audio-derived teacher supervision yields modest but consistent gains over contour-only training, indicating that phonological information from synchronized audio can be partially transferred to articulatory models. Finally, posterior analyses show interpretable surface-sensitive patterns consistent with flapping-like /t/ realizations, /t/-/r/ retraction or affrication, and nasal place assimilation.

</details>


### [98] [PragMatch: Separating Pragmatic Incongruity from Cross-Modal Mismatch in Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09772)
*Zhanna Mukhametsharip,Vera Demberg,Varsha Suresh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了PragMatch，一个基于MMSD2.0构建的3,000对图像-文本受控基准，用于系统评估大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）在多模态讽刺检测中是否依赖表面捷径（如词汇、OCR和风格线索）而非真正的多模态语用推理。实验表明，注入表面信号会大幅改变模型预测，尽管图像-文本间关系未变，揭示了当前LVLMs在多模态语用推理方面的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型在多模态基准上表现优异，但其是否真正理解图像与文本之间的关系仍不明确——模型可能依赖表面相关性（即捷径学习）而非深层推理。这一问题在多模态讽刺检测中尤为关键，因为讽刺检测需要识别语用上的不一致（pragmatic incongruity），而非简单地判断图像-文本不匹配。现有基准缺乏对模型是否走捷径的系统性评估手段。

Method: 构建PragMatch基准：从MMSD2.0中派生3,000对图像-文本对，包括原始讽刺样本以及构造的字面（literal）对和困难负样本（hard-negative）对。通过系统性掩码（masking）识别有影响力的捷径线索（包括词汇、OCR提取文本和风格特征），并通过针对性的注入实验（injection experiments）评估这些线索对模型预测的影响。

Result: 实验发现LVLMs的预测对词汇线索、OCR-derived线索和风格线索高度敏感。在保持图像-文本关系不变的情况下，注入表面信号导致模型预测发生显著变化，表明模型确实依赖表面捷径而非真正的多模态语用推理。

Conclusion: 当前LVLMs在多模态讽刺检测中存在依赖表面线索的局限性，而非真正进行多模态语用推理。PragMatch为评估超越表面图像-文本对齐的多模态语用推理能力提供了一个系统性测试平台。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have demonstrated strong performance on multimodal benchmarks, yet it remains unclear whether they genuinely reason about relationships between images and text or rely on superficial correlations, known as shortcut learning. This question is particularly important for multimodal sarcasm detection, where successful prediction depends on recognizing pragmatic incongruity rather than treating sarcasm as simple image-text mismatch. We introduce PragMatch, a controlled benchmark of 3,000 image-text pairs derived from MMSD2.0, including original sarcastic examples and constructed literal and hard-negative pairs. We identify influential shortcut cues through systematic masking and evaluate their impact through targeted injection experiments. Our results show that LVLM predictions are sensitive to lexical, OCR-derived and stylistic cues, with injected surface signals causing substantial changes in model predictions despite unchanged underlying image-text relationships. Our findings reveal limitations in current LVLMs while PragMatch provides a systematic testbed for evaluating multimodal pragmatic reasoning beyond surface-level image-text alignment.

</details>


### [99] [KGCaRe: Explainable Complex Conditional Question Answering using Automatic Knowledge Graph Construction and Context Retrieval with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09779)
*Ghanshyam Verma,Simanta Sarkar,Devishree Pillai,Hotaka Shiokawa,Yourong Xu,Fiona Veazey,Peter Hubbert,Hui Su,Paul Buitelaar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: KGCaRe是一种混合RAG方法，通过LLM生成的KG（知识图谱）结合神经检索与符号推理，处理复杂条件问答。它从文档中构建KG并存入图数据库，同时将文档嵌入向量库进行神经检索，通过LLM引导的迭代图遍历提取相关三元组并结合检索文本段落生成带解释的答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用LLM和RAG在特定领域的复杂条件问答任务中表现不佳，作者假设将非结构化知识（文档）和结构化知识（KG）结合到RAG中可以提升推理和回答准确性。

Method: KGCaRe采用混合架构：1) 使用多提示策略从文档中构建KG并存储到图数据库；2) 将文档嵌入向量库实现神经检索；3) 通过LLM引导的迭代图遍历提取相关三元组并剪枝无关信息，若初始遍历上下文不足则利用额外线索实体再次遍历；4) 将KG路径形式的三元组与语义检索文本段落合并，通过定制提示生成带解释的答案。

Result: 在两个复杂条件QA数据集上，KGCaRe在Mistral、Mixtral、GPT-3.5和GPT-4o等多个LLM上一致优于Vanilla LLM、Code Prompt、Text Prompt、Think-on-Graph、Vanilla RAG和HybridContextQA等基线方法。

Conclusion: 结合KG结构化知识与神经检索的混合RAG方法能显著提升复杂条件问答的准确性，KGCaRe验证了该假设并公开了实现软件管线。

Abstract: Answering complex conditional questions using Large Language Models (LLMs) and Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) remains a challenge, particularly in domain-specific contexts where general-purpose LLMs and RAG tend to underperform. We hypothesize that augmenting RAG with unstructured and structured knowledge, extracted from both documents and knowledge graphs (KGs), can improve reasoning and answer accuracy for such tasks.
  To test this, we propose KGCaRe, a hybrid approach that combines neural retrieval with symbolic reasoning over LLM-generated KGs. KGCaRe constructs a KG from documents using a multi-prompt extraction strategy and stores it in a graph database. Simultaneously, the documents are embedded into a vector store to enable neural retrieval. KGCaRe performs innovative iterative graph traversal guided by the LLM to extract relevant triples, prune irrelevant information, and uses additional clue entities to traverse the graph again if the initial traversal does not provide satisfactory context to generate the answer. The relevant triples extracted from the KG in path form, along with semantically retrieved text passages, are then fed into custom KGCaRe prompts to generate answers to the complex conditional questions with explanations.
  We evaluate KGCaRe on two complex conditional QA datasets. Our results on these datasets show that KGCaRe consistently outperforms existing baselines, including Vanilla LLM, Code Prompt, Text Prompt, Think-on-Graph, Vanilla RAG, and HybridContextQA, across multiple LLMs such as Mistral, Mixtral, GPT-3.5, and GPT-4o. We publicly release the software pipeline that we developed to implement the proposed KGCaRe approach.

</details>


### [100] [Comparing British and American Audio Description of Movies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09792)
*Igor Sterner,Alex Lascarides,Frank Keller*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对英国和美国创作的电影音频描述（audio description）进行了定量比较分析，利用两地区编写的指南和从业者的实践经验提出假设，并在包含206部电影的双语人工音频描述语料库上进行验证，最终证实了包括词汇、进行体、被动结构、主观形容词、命名时机、场景提示方式以及与对白和音乐的重复度等方面的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频描述作为一种让盲人和视障人士能够追随电影故事情节的叙事技术，其受到极大的约束：不仅需要传达电影中的故事，还必须嵌入对白之间的空隙，并符合各地区制定的指南。此前缺乏对英国和美国音频描述之间系统性差异的定量研究，作者希望通过实证方法揭示这些差异。

Method: 作者利用英国和美国两个地区的音频描述指南，并结合一位从业者的实践经验，提出关于两地区音频描述差异的具体假设。然后在既有语料库上进行验证，该语料库包含206部电影，每部电影均有美国人和英国人分别撰写的人工音频描述，对假设进行定量检验。

Result: 所有测试的假设均得到定量证据的支持，涵盖多个维度：词汇选择、进行体（progressive aspect）的使用、被动结构的运用、主观形容词和修饰语的使用、角色命名的时机、场景提示（scene cueing）的方式，以及与电影对白和音乐的重叠程度等方面均存在显著的地区性差异。

Conclusion: 本文为音频描述这一叙事技术提供了定量的研究视角，首次以大规模实证方式揭示了英国与美国音频描述在语言和叙事策略上的系统性差异，为未来音频描述研究、实践和指南制定提供了有价值的参考。

Abstract: Narrating the visual component of movies is known as audio description. It is a narrative technique designed to enable blind and visually impaired individuals to follow the story. However, it is far more constrained than most narratives: the descriptions not only need to convey the story in the movie, but they must also fit into gaps between dialogue and they need to conform to guidelines that exist in each region. In this work, we compare audio description created in the United Kingdom against audio description created in the United States. We use guidelines written for these two regions, alongside the impressions from a practitioner in the field, to motivate specific hypotheses about the differences. We test these hypotheses against our pre-existing corpus, which provides both human-authored American and British audio description for each of 206 movies. Results provide quantitative evidence to uphold all tested hypotheses, including differences in lexicon, the use of the progressive aspect, the use of passive constructions, the use of subjective adjectives and modifiers, when characters are named, how scenes are cued, and degree of overlap with movie dialogue and music. Our work offers a quantitative lens into the narrative technique of audio description.

</details>


### [101] [SWE-Bench ProMax: Benchmarking Agents on Large-Scale Multilingual Code Refactoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09802)
*Yuling Shi,Jinghan Xu,Kelin Fu,Wenhao Zeng,Shilin He,Lei Zhang,Yue Liu,Zelin Zhao,Terry Yue Zhuo,Jialun Cao,Siyu Ye,Tianyu Liu,Kai Cai,Shing-Chi Cheung,Xiaodong Gu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SWE-Bench ProMax是一个专家策展的多语言代码重构基准，包含170个来自七个编程语言真实提交的实例。通过严格的策展流程（重写问题描述、手动审查测试套件、过滤低复杂度任务），该基准平均涉及11.4个文件和261.6行代码，远超现有基准规模。实验表明最先进模型在两种agent框架下仅达到41.2%的解决率，是一个尚未饱和的挑战性基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI编码智能体基准正迅速饱和，且评估质量受到严重质疑：一项审计发现SWE-bench Verified中近60%未解决实例存在缺陷测试（过窄或过宽的测试），且前沿模型可从训练数据中逐字复制金牌补丁。代码重构需要跨多文件的协调、行为保持的更改，是更难且更真实的智能体能力测试，但现有基准对此覆盖不足。

Method: 构建了SWE-Bench ProMax基准：(1) 从七个编程语言（Python、Java、TypeScript、Go、C、C++、Rust）的真实提交中筛选170个重构成实例；(2) 采用多阶段严格策展流程，从零重写问题描述以提供精确无歧义的规格说明；(3) 手动审查测试套件，移除过窄和过宽的测试；(4) 过滤复杂度不足或跨文件范围有限的实例；(5) 在两种agent框架下使用前沿模型进行实验评估。

Result: SWE-Bench ProMax上的实例平均涉及11.4个修改文件和261.6行代码，规模显著超过现有基准。在两种agent scaffold下的前沿模型实验中，最佳模型仅达到41.2%的解决率，证明该基准对当前AI编码智能体构成了有意义且未饱和的挑战。

Conclusion: SWE-Bench ProMax是首个大规模、专家策展的多语言代码重构基准，直接回应了现有基准的质量缺陷，为评估AI编码智能体在复杂、长跨度软件工程任务上的能力提供了更严格和更现实的测试平台。基准已公开发布。

Abstract: As AI coding agents take on increasingly complex, long-horizon software engineering tasks, existing benchmarks are rapidly saturating and their evaluation quality has come under serious scrutiny: a recent audit found that nearly 60% of unsolved SWE-bench Verified instances contain flawed tests -- either overly narrow tests that reject correct solutions or overly broad tests that check unstated requirements -- and that frontier models can verbatim reproduce gold patches from training data. Code refactoring, which requires coordinated, behavior-preserving changes across many files, offers a substantially harder and more realistic test of agent capability, yet remains underserved by current benchmarks. We introduce SWE-Bench ProMax, an expert-curated, multilingual code refactoring benchmark of 170 instances drawn from real commits across seven programming languages (Python, Java, TypeScript, Go, C, C++, and Rust). Every instance undergoes rigorous, multi-stage curation that directly addresses the quality problems identified in prior benchmarks: issue descriptions are rewritten from scratch to provide precise, unambiguous specifications, and test suites are manually reviewed to remove overly narrow and overly broad tests. Tasks with insufficient complexity or limited cross-file scope are filtered out, yielding a benchmark of challenging, large-scale refactoring tasks that average 11.4 modified files and 261.6 lines of code per instance, substantially exceeding the scale of existing benchmarks. Experiments with frontier models under two agent scaffolds show that the best model achieves only 41.2% resolve rate, confirming that SWE-Bench ProMax presents a meaningful and unsaturated challenge for current AI coding agents. Our benchmark is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/swe-bench-promax/SWE-Bench-ProMax.

</details>


### [102] [RA-FinBERT: Rule-aware LoRA adaptation for low-resource financial sentiment classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09834)
*Fan Zhang,Jiaming Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出RA-FinBERT，一种参数高效的金融情感分析框架，将LoRA微调的FinBERT与VADER规则特征及来源元数据结合，仅增加1,024个可训练参数即可提升三分类情感分析的准确率和宏F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有资源高效的金融NLP研究主要集中于压缩或适配预训练语言模型，缺乏将上下文表示与轻量级规则特征相结合的研究。在计算资源受限的环境下，需要一种既能利用预训练模型语义能力、又能以最小额外复杂度提升性能的方法。

Method: RA-FinBERT包含三个关键组件：(1) 使用LoRA（低秩适配）微调FinBERT；(2) 将VADER推导的连续情感比例（积极、消极、中性）和来源级元数据特征构成标准化的四维特征向量；(3) 将四维特征向量与FinBERT最终层768维[CLS]表示拼接，通过轻量级分类头进行分类，仅增加1,024个可训练权重。

Result: 在金融新闻标题和描述的测试集上，RA-FinBERT达到69.89%准确率和0.634宏F1，而纯文本FinBERT为63.44%准确率和0.526宏F1。中性类别召回率从18.18%提升至45.45%。

Conclusion: 规则派生情感信息和来源元数据能够为上下文FinBERT表示提供互补信号，以极小的模型复杂度换取显著的性能提升，支持CPU和GPU执行，适合计算资源受限的实际应用场景。

Abstract: Financial sentiment analysis converts unstructured financial news into quantitative signals that can support market analysis and decision-making. Existing work on resource-efficient financial NLP has largely focused on compressing or adapting pretrained language models, with less attention to combining contextual representations with lightweight rule-derived features. This study develops Rule-Aware FinBERT (RA-FinBERT), a parameter-efficient framework that integrates low-rank adaptation (LoRA) with three continuous VADER-derived sentiment proportions (positive, negative, and neutral) and a source-level metadata feature. The standardized four-dimensional feature vector is directly concatenated with the 768-dimensional final-layer FinBERT [CLS] representation and passed through a lightweight classification head. This design introduces only 1,024 additional trainable weights relative to a structurally matched text-only FinBERT model. RA-FinBERT was evaluated against text-only FinBERT and a lightweight DistilBERT baseline for three-class sentiment classification of financial-news titles and descriptions. On the held-out test set, RA-FinBERT achieved 69.89% accuracy and a macro F1 score of 0.634, compared with 63.44% and 0.526 for text-only FinBERT. Neutral-class recall increased from 18.18% to 45.45%. The framework supports both CPU and GPU execution, offering a lightweight and practical approach to financial sentiment classification under constrained computational resources. These findings indicate that rule-derived sentiment information and source metadata can provide complementary signals to contextual FinBERT representations and improve performance with minimal additional model complexity.

</details>


### [103] [Fusion Training for Mathematical Generalization in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09893)
*Congfeng Cao,Pengyu Zhang,Jelke Bloem*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了Thinking Mode Fusion（TMF）中两种模式（非思考模式与思考模式）之间的数据比例和训练调度对模型性能的影响，发现两模式之间存在非对称交互和负相关张力，并提供了实用的TMF训练配置指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TMF能够在单一模型中同时支持简洁响应（非思考模式）和长格式推理（思考模式），但其训练动力学（包括数据比例和训练调度）尚未被充分探索，缺乏系统性的理解和指导。

Method: 聚焦数学问题求解任务，构建了包含多种思考/非思考数据比例和三种训练调度的基准测试集，系统分析训练调度和数据比例对TMF模型性能的影响。

Result: 发现两种模式之间存在非对称交互：增加非思考监督数据的比例会降低思考模式的准确率；不同训练调度会调节这种权衡，且最优调度取决于数据比例；非思考模式与思考模式的监督之间存在负相关，揭示了两种模式之间的固有张力。

Conclusion: 这些发现为设计有效的TMF训练方案提供了实践指导，所有代码和数据已公开发布以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Thinking Mode Fusion (TMF) enables large language models to support both concise responses and long-form reasoning by unifying a non-thinking mode and a thinking mode within a single model. However, its training dynamics, including the \emph{data ratio} and \emph{training schedule} between the two modes, remain underexplored. In this work, we present a systematic study of TMF by analyzing the effects of the training schedule and data ratio between thinking and non-thinking modes. Focusing on mathematical problem solving, we construct a benchmark with multiple thinking-to-non-thinking data ratios and three training schedules. Our results reveal an asymmetric interaction between the two modes: increasing the ratio of non-thinking supervision reduces the accuracy of the thinking mode. We further show that different training schedules modulate this trade-off and that the optimal schedule depends on the data ratio. Finally, we quantify a negative correlation between non-thinking and thinking mode supervision, highlighting an inherent tension between these two modes. These findings provide practical guidance for designing effective TMF training settings. All code and data are released to support further research at: \href{https://github.com/caocongfeng/Fusion-Bench.git}{\textbf{Fusion Bench}}.

</details>


### [104] [Consilience for Verifier-Free Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09898)
*Lecheng Kong,Like Hui,Haitao Mao,Jun Huan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出consilience框架，通过显式评估推理过程中置信度的时序不对称性（低初始置信度、高最终置信度）来解决现有基于置信度的无验证器测试时扩展（VF-TTS）方法在复杂任务上崩溃的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于置信度的VF-TTS方法在复杂任务上会灾难性失效，因为均匀高置信度往往表明探索失败，倾向于给出自信但错误的答案。需要一种新的置信度评估方式来捕捉健康的认知搜索模式。

Method: 提出consilience选择框架，显式评估推理中置信度的时序不对称性。通过一个组合度量来主动惩罚高初始置信度，同时严格要求最终确定性，从而实现探索分支向收敛解的过渡。

Result: 在研究生级别数学问题和自由形式代码生成任务上的大量实验表明，consilience有效优于现有基线方法，验证了关于完成置信度这一新视角的有效性。

Conclusion: 健康的认知搜索应呈现特定的置信度轨迹模式：初始低置信度驱动探索性分支，最终高置信度收敛到解决方案。基于此洞察的consilience框架为无验证器环境下的测试时扩展提供了更稳健的置信度评估方法。

Abstract: Test-time scaling often uses an external verifier, such as compilers and test cases in coding or trained value functions in robotics applications, to obtain high-quality rollouts. Verifier-free test-time scaling (or VF-TTS) is gaining extensive attention as a mechanism to enhance Large Language Model (LLM) reasoning, primarily because we do not have access to such high-quality verifiers in many real-world applications. Among existing VF-TTS methods, confidence-based VF-TTS methods, which compute and rank rollouts solely by confidence, are particularly promising. Such methods introduce near-zero overhead for sample evaluation and require minimal access to internal model states, making the methods highly flexible across models and tasks.
  In this paper, we demonstrate a critical limitation of existing confidence-based VF-TTS methods by showing that such methods catastrophically break down on complex tasks. We observe a very interesting phenomenon: uniformly high confidence frequently indicates a failure to explore, favoring confidently wrong answers. To address this, our core insight is that robust cognitive search requires a specific confidence trajectory pattern: such methods perform exploratory branching at the beginning, as manifested by low initial confidence, and converge to a high final confidence solution. To implement this insight, we introduce consilience, a novel selection framework that explicitly evaluates the temporal asymmetry of confidence in reasoning. We operationalize this via a combinatorial metric that actively penalizes high initial confidence while strictly demanding final certainty. Extensive experiments covering both graduate-level mathematics problems and free-form code generation demonstrate that consilience effectively outperforms existing baselines, validating our novel perspective on completion confidence.

</details>


### [105] [Decoding-Level Taboo: A Diagnostic Stress Test for LLM Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09900)
*Tadanobu Chuyo Kamijo,Ori Rottenstreich,Javier Conde,Gonzalo Martínez,Pedro Reviriego*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Decoding-Level Taboo，一种零提示（zero-prompt）诊断性压力测试方法，通过在logit空间运行时动态屏蔽词边界的主候选token，强制模型偏离其最优生成路径，从而揭示大型语言模型在非名义条件下的真实鲁棒性。实验表明，离路径鲁棒性受参数规模和指令对齐程度影响，且该方法可作为一种生成合成数据、压力测试安全护栏和审计模型可靠性的通用原语。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型评估主要关注名义（nominal）条件下的性能表现，这造成了一种能力幻觉——模型在高度优化的生成路径上表现良好，但在真实部署场景中，复杂系统提示、安全护栏和结构约束会持续将模型推出这一名义路径，导致基准分数与实际部署表现之间存在严重分歧。

Method: 提出Decoding-Level Taboo，一种零提示诊断性压力测试方法。该方法不需要构造任何提示语，而是直接在运行时干预logit空间：在词边界上动态屏蔽主要候选token，从而强制模型进行"迂回表达"（circumlocution），使模型偏离其惯常的生成路径。

Result: 在多种开源权重模型家族上的评估显示：离路径鲁棒性与参数规模和训练后的指令对齐程度显著相关，模型越大、对齐越好，鲁棒性通常越强。

Conclusion: Taboo不仅是一种评估工具，更提供了一种新的原语（primitive），可用于生成多样化合成数据集、压力测试运行时安全护栏以及在真实部署前审计模型可靠性。

Abstract: Large language model evaluations typically focus on performance under nominal conditions, creating an illusion of capability where models comfortably walk a narrow, highly optimized generation corridor. In real-world deployments, however, complex system prompts, safety guardrails, and structural constraints continuously force models off this nominal path, driving a divergence between benchmark scores and deployment performance. To address this issue, we introduce Decoding-Level Taboo, a zero-prompt diagnostic stress test that intervenes directly in logit space at runtime, forcing models out of their nominal paths. By dynamically masking primary candidate tokens at word boundaries, Taboo forces machine circumlocution.
  Evaluating Taboo across several open-weight model families reveals that off-path robustness is heavily influenced by both parameter scale and post-training instruction alignment, with robustness generally improving with model size and alignment. Beyond the results presented in this paper, Taboo provides a novel primitive for generating diverse synthetic datasets, stress-testing runtime safety guardrails, and auditing model reliability prior to real-world deployment.

</details>


### [106] [From Values to Benchmarks: Evaluating Large Language Models for Governmental Use in Dutch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09925)
*Laurens Samson,Iva Gornishka,Gossa Lô,Yuki M. Asano,Sennay Ghebreab*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"Grip on LLMs"框架，这是一个针对荷兰政府使用的系统化大型语言模型评估套件，由市政领域专家协作开发。该框架识别出六个评估维度（事实性、诚实性、社会偏见、能耗、成本、训练数据透明度），并在30多个多语言和荷兰语专用模型上进行基准测试。结果表明没有任何单一模型在所有维度上表现优异，质量提升必然伴随更高的环境与财务成本，且事实性与诚实性由不同机制支配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型越来越多地被部署在政府环境中，但现有评估框架很少同时反映公共行政的价值取向和非英语语境的语言要求，导致政府在模型选型时缺乏系统性的评估工具。

Method: 通过与荷兰大型市政组织的领域专家合作，采用咨询委员会流程、用户研究和公务员聊天机器人用户调查，识别出六个评估维度，并将其操作化为覆盖30多个多语言和荷兰语专用模型的基准测试套件。

Result: 没有任何单一模型在所有维度上均表现优异；质量与能耗及成本之间存在不可避免的权衡；偏见与质量和成本相对独立；高事实性不意味着高诚实性，二者由不同属性主导。

Conclusion: 该框架为政府LLM选择提供了可操作的评估工具，并发布了面向非技术受众的公开模型概览，服务于从工程师到政策制定者的全范围利益相关者。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly being deployed in governmental settings, yet few existing evaluation frameworks jointly reflect the values of public administration and the linguistic requirements of non-English contexts. We present the "Grip on LLMs" framework, a systematic evaluation suite for Dutch governmental use developed in collaboration with domain experts from a major Dutch municipal organisation. Through an advisory board process, user research, and a survey of the users of a civil-servant chatbot, we identify six evaluation dimensions (factuality, honesty, social bias, energy consumption, cost, and training data transparency) and operationalise them into a benchmark suite covering more than 30 multilingual and Dutch-specific models. Our results reveal that no single model excels across all dimensions, and that trade-offs are unavoidable: higher quality consistently comes at greater environmental impact and financial cost, while bias remains largely independent of both. We further find that factuality (whether a model answers correctly) and honesty (whether a model acknowledges what it does not know) are governed by distinct properties, with high factuality not implying high honesty. To make these findings actionable for non-technical audiences, we release a publicly accessible, user-friendly model overview designed for the full range of stakeholders involved in governmental LLM selection, from engineers to policymakers.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [107] [Zero-Trust Federated Learning for Connected Aftermarket Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07591)
*Shunmukha Sagar Puppala*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ZT FL CADE——一种面向联网售后设备（如汽车后装诊断设备）的零信任联邦学习边缘架构，将设备级访问控制、隐私保护的联邦学习和对抗性验证相结合，用于OTA更新和预测性维护决策。在包含144,000个遥测窗口（240台设备、12个供应商域、180天）的合成数据集上，该方法在维护风险F1（0.837→0.883）、入侵检测F1（0.856→0.897）等方面优于FedAvg，且当20%客户端为对抗性时仍保持0.842的入侵召回率，平均访问决策延迟为44ms（低于100ms预算）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联网售后设备（connected aftermarket devices）延伸了车辆诊断、维修工作流和OTA软件维护的范围，超越了原始设备制造商（OEM）的边界，但其所有权异构、使用寿命长的特点使传统的外围安全模型难以适用，亟需新的安全架构来应对这一挑战。

Method: ZT FL CADE是一种边缘学习架构，包含：①设备级访问控制；②隐私保护的联邦学习（训练局部时序模型并聚合隐私受限的更新）；③基于行为与更新完整性证据的对抗性验证——为每台设备打分；④将更新请求路由为允许（allow）、挑战（challenge）或隔离（quarantine）三种动作。数据集特征包括总线熵、更新延迟、证明年龄、签名重试、环境信号、故障码率、丢包、漂移、里程和信任分数，目标包括维护风险、更新入侵和访问动作。

Result: 在合成实验中：维护风险F1从FedAvg的0.837提升至0.883；入侵检测F1从0.856提升至0.897；当20%所选客户端为对抗性时，入侵召回率保持0.842；平均访问决策延迟为44ms，低于模拟中100ms的操作预算。

Conclusion: 虽然结果尚未建立实地验证，但研究表明零信任策略执行和联邦学习可以作为一个联合的整体进行评估，而非作为独立的售后市场安全控制分别处理，从而为售后联网设备的安全更新与维护提供了新的架构思路。

Abstract: Connected aftermarket devices extend vehicle diagnostics, repair workflows, and over-the-air software maintenance beyond original equipment manufacturer boundaries, yet their heterogeneous ownership and long service life complicate conventional perimeter security. This paper develops Zero Trust Federated Learning for Connected Aftermarket Devices (ZT FL CADE), an edge-learning architecture that combines device-level access control, privacy-preserving federated learning, and adversarial validation for over-the-air update and predictive maintenance decisions. The evaluation uses a single synthetic dataset of 144,000 telemetry windows from 240 devices, 12 vendor domains, and 180 days of operation. Features include bus entropy, update latency, attestation age, signature retries, environmental signals, fault-code rates, packet loss, drift, mileage, and trust score, with targets for maintenance risk, update intrusion, and access action. ZT FL CADE trains local temporal models, aggregates privacy-bounded updates, scores each device against behavioral and update integrity evidence, and routes update requests to allow, challenge, or quarantine actions. Synthetic experiments improve maintenance risk F1 from 0.837 for Fed Avg to 0.883, improve intrusion F1 from 0.856 to 0.897, and preserve 0.842 intrusion recall when 20 percent of selected clients are adversarial. Mean access-decision latency remains 44 MS, below the 100 MS operational budget used in the simulation. The results do not establish field validation, but they indicate that zero-trust policy enforcement and federated learning can be evaluated jointly rather than as separate aftermarket security controls. Index Terms Zero trust architecture, federated learning, connected aftermarket devices, over-the-air updates, adversarial machine learning, edge artificial intelligence, predictive maintenance, automotive cybersecurity

</details>


### [108] [Adversarial Attacks on Deep OCR Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07636)
*Wenbo Sun,Hongzong LI,Yanyun Wang,Jiahao MA,Shuxin Zhuang,Rong Feng,Shiqin Tang,Zi Liang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次提出针对生成式OCR视觉语言模型（Deep-OCR）的纯黑盒对抗攻击方法，仅依赖解码后的字符串输出，通过零阶优化和随机方向有限差分估计梯度，实现无目标与有目标攻击，并在初步实验中暴露了严重的解码器缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Deep-OCR将视觉模态视为光学压缩媒介，实现了低token成本的长上下文OCR，但其复杂性的增加可能引入新的安全漏洞。目前尚无人研究针对此类生成式OCR视觉语言模型的对抗攻击，尤其缺乏仅依赖字符串输出的纯黑盒攻击方法。

Method: 将攻击建模为零阶优化问题，通过序列相似度直接定义在字符串输出上的有界标量损失；采用随机方向有限差分方案估计梯度，其查询成本与图像维度无关；使用Adam更新结合无穷范数投影生成不可察觉的扰动，支持无目标与有目标两种攻击目标。

Result: 初步实验验证了仅基于字符串的攻击与评估流程的有效性，并暴露了严重的解码器定性失败，包括重复、截断和提示泄露问题；同时发现受控的目标改写攻击显著难于无目标降级攻击，因此暂不宣称目标攻击成功，等待预注册评估完成。

Conclusion: 该研究首次实现了对生成式OCR模型的纯黑盒对抗攻击，证明了此类模型存在可被利用的安全漏洞，且仅通过查询解码字符串即可实现有效攻击；目标攻击的困难性表明需要更全面的评估来确认其可行性。

Abstract: Deep-OCR (DeepSeek-OCR) advances document recognition by treating the visual modality as an optical compression medium, enabling long-context OCR at low token cost. However, its increased complexity may introduce new security vulnerabilities. In this paper, we present, to the best of our knowledge, the first pure black-box adversarial attack against a generative OCR vision-language model, where only the decoded string can be queried and no gradients, logits, or model internals are available. We recast the attack as a zeroth-order optimization problem driven by a bounded scalar loss defined directly on the string output via sequence similarity, and estimate the gradient with a random-direction finite-difference scheme whose query cost is independent of the image dimension. An Adam update with ell_infinity projection yields imperceptible perturbations for both untargeted and targeted objectives. Pilot experiments on Deep-OCR validate the string-only attack and evaluation pipeline and expose severe qualitative decoder failures, including repetition, truncation, and prompt leakage. They also show that controlled targeted rewriting remains substantially harder than untargeted degradation; we avoid claiming targeted success until the pre-registered evaluation is complete.

</details>


### [109] [Can AI Write Compliant Code, and to What Extent? Evaluating SOC 2 Compliance of Claude Fable 5, Claude Opus 4.8, and Claude Opus 5 Across Four Use Cases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07776)
*Iccha Sethi,Herman Errico*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究测试了三个前沿大语言模型（Claude Fable 5、Opus 4.8、Opus 5）在四个生产代码用例（S3 CLI、认证服务、RDS Terraform模块、文件上传处理器）中，是否能在未被明确要求时自动遵循SOC 2标准的安全控制（加密、访问限制、日志、保留）。无提示时合规率仅47%-88%，且仅覆盖"代码常规写法"内建的控制（如密码哈希、storage_encrypted），而S3加固、保留策略、MFA钩子等缺失，甚至产出可利用的远程代码执行漏洞。仅添加一句SOC 2提示后，合规率升至86%-100%，所有不安全构造均被消除，但模型任务概念之外的控制（MFA钩子、cookie标志、账户生命周期）仍然缺失。模型间差异最小，而模式匹配评分器不可靠（与语义评分有27/216处分歧），需要替换为语义检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件团队正将包括存储、凭证处理和受监管数据在内的生产代码委托给语言模型，但没有人明确提及安全时，模型是否会自动应用SOC 2项目所期望的控制（加密、受限访问、日志、保留）尚不清楚。一句提及标准的话能带来多大改变也需量化。

Method: 测试三个前沿模型、四个用例（S3 CLI、认证服务、RDS Terraform模块、文件上传处理器），每个用例分别用中性任务描述和添加一句SOC 2标准描述的提示各生成一次，共24个输出，对照映射到特定信任服务标准（TSC）的二元评分细则进行评分，并人工核查所有失败与标记行为。

Result: 无提示合规率47%-88%，且与"该控制是否为代码常规写法"相关：密码哈希和storage_encrypted无需提示即可出现，但S3加固调用、保留策略和MFA钩子不会。中性提示产生了真实漏洞（可远程代码执行的Werkzeug调试器、未认证下载、返回所有姓名邮箱的端点）。一句SOC 2提示使所有用例达标86%-100%，弥补23-50分，消除所有不安全构造，但超出模型任务认知的控制（MFA钩子、cookie标志、账户生命周期）仍缺失。模型间差异最小，模式匹配评分器与语义评分在27/216处不一致并放过了真实缺陷。

Conclusion: 前沿模型在无人提示时不会完整应用SOC 2控制，仅覆盖代码约定俗成的部分；一句显式提及标准的提示能显著提升合规性并消除已知漏洞，但无法覆盖模型任务概念之外的控制。模型选择差异不大，且需用语义检查替代模式匹配评分器。

Abstract: Software teams now delegate production code to language models, including code that provisions storage, handles credentials, and stores regulated data, so we asked whether a model applies the controls a SOC~2 program expects (encryption, restricted access, logging, retention) when nobody mentions security, and how much one sentence naming the standard changes the answer. We tested three frontier models (Claude Fable~5, Opus~4.8, and Opus~5) across four use cases (an S3 CLI, an authentication service, an RDS Terraform module, and a file-upload handler holding personal data), each generated once from a neutral task statement and once with a single SOC~2 sentence added, scoring all 24 outputs against binary rubrics mapped to specific Trust Services Criteria and hand-verifying every failure and flagged act. Unprompted conformance ran from 47\% to 88\% and tracked whether a control is part of how the code is normally written, so password hashing and \texttt{storage\_encrypted} appear unasked while S3 hardening calls, retention, and MFA hooks do not. The neutral prompt also shipped real vulnerabilities, including a reachable Werkzeug debugger allowing remote code execution, an unauthenticated download, and an endpoint returning every stored name and email, all scored clean by our first checklist, with a fourth defect passing because its value was computed by a conditional. One SOC~2 sentence moved every case to 86--100\%, worth 23 to 50 points, and removed every insecure construction, though controls outside the model's conception of the task survived it, including MFA hooks, cookie flags, and account lifecycle. Model choice mattered least, with same-generation models within one rubric item across all eight cells, and the pattern-matching scorer proved unreliable, disagreeing with semantic grading on 27 of 216 judgments and passing a real defect, so it needs replacing with semantic checks.

</details>


### [110] [The Anatomy of a Prompt Injection: A Component Model for Structured Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07808)
*Jeremy McHugh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对2022年首次被发现以来仍然以字符串而非结构化形式记录的快速注入攻击，提出了一个七组件模型来形式化描述快速注入工件，使防御方、红队人员及网络威胁情报团队能够标注、比较和变异攻击，而无需依赖脆弱的字符串匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速注入攻击自2022年被发现四年以来，仍然主要以逐字字符串的形式被记录，而非结构化漏洞利用，这阻碍了防御方和威胁情报团队对攻击进行系统化分析、比较和变异。由于大语言模型会将不同的自然语言表述编译为相同的可执行行为，仅依赖表面措辞的字符串匹配是不够的，必须跟踪攻击者意图（工具目标、汇点和效果）。

Method: 提出一个七组件模型来形式化快速注入工件，包括五个工件字段（载体、交付向量、隐藏方式、上下文中断、特权提升、载荷和回传通道）和两个环境字段。该框架统一了HOUYI的载荷分解、Promptware Kill Chain和活动分类法中的部分角色，并将ReNeLLM等最小越狱框架视为在受限子空间上的投影。提供清晰的标注规则、直接映射到行业CTI模式的逻辑分析记录、包含EchoLeak（CVE-2025-32711）和野外恶意软件AI规避样本的实例，以及说明性智能体流程图。

Result: 该框架提供了统一的标注规则和逻辑分析记录，可直接映射到行业CTI模式，并通过EchoLeak（CVE-2025-32711）和野外恶意软件AI规避样本等实际案例以及智能体流程图进行验证。

Conclusion: 通过将快速注入攻击结构化为七组件模型，使防御者、红队人员和CTI团队能够在不依赖脆弱的字符串匹配的情况下对攻击进行有效标注、比较和变异，从而提升对快速注入威胁的防御、检测和情报分析能力。

Abstract: Four years after prompt injection was first identified in 2022, attacks are still predominantly documented as verbatim strings rather than structured exploits, despite advancing agent capabilities and threat actors embedding injections to subvert AI-assisted security analysis. This paper formalizes the structure of prompt-injection artifacts, enabling defenders, red teamers, and cyber threat intelligence (CTI) teams to label, compare, and mutate attacks without relying on fragile string matching. Because large language models compile varied natural-language realizations into identical executable actions, labeling must track attacker intent (tool targets, sinks, and effects) rather than surface wording. We propose a seven-component model (carrier, delivery vector, concealment, context-break, privilege escalation, payload, and return channel) consisting of five artifact fields and two environment fields. This framework unifies roles partially addressed by HOUYI's payload decomposition, the Promptware Kill Chain, and campaign taxonomies, while framing minimal jailbreak frameworks like ReNeLLM as projections onto a restricted subspace. We provide clear labeling rules, a logical analysis record mapping directly to industry CTI schemas, worked examples including EchoLeak (CVE-2025-32711) and an in-the-wild malware AI-evasion sample, and an illustrative agentic flowchart.

</details>


### [111] [China RealDID: Verifiable Credentials Anchored in Legal Identity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07846)
*Yifan He*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出China RealDID，一个三层架构（CTID中心化法定身份、RealDID去中心化锚定、基于SD-JWT的可选择性披露VC），通过内容盲化的政府中继机制，在保留可验证凭证选择性披露能力的同时实现法律锚定，构建了不对等的、状态受限的信任模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可验证凭证（VC）和去中心化标识符（DID）虽然支持选择性披露，但缺乏法律锚定——没有可信的身份根，验证者无法区分真实持证人与伪造身份。而国家身份系统提供生物特征验证，但引入三种成本：验证者需收集完整个人信息、基础设施单一API集中、国家观察每笔交易。两者的张力和冲突是本文要解决的核心问题。

Method: 提出三层架构：CTID（中心化法定身份层）作为身份根，RealDID（开放许可区块链上的去中心化锚定层），以及基于SD-JWT的VC选择性披露层。核心机制是"内容盲化政府中继"：国家验证参与者并对每个凭证做副署签名，但由于凭证由签发者用持有者公钥加密，政府无法读取负载内容。论文还描述了VC生命周期、三重签名链、开放模板注册表以及架构的元数据级隐私限制（包括中继处的凭证图问题和单DID复用导致的表现链接性）。

Result: 该设计产生了一种不对称的、状态受限的信任模型：政府无法冒充用户或读取内容，但用户无法伪造身份或规避元数据观察。论文分析了与中国《个人信息保护法》和欧盟GDPR的合规性（包括不可变注册表与删除权之间的张力），并探讨了通过新加坡和香港的跨境部署实现泛化。

Conclusion: China RealDID提供了一个介于中心化法定身份与去中心化可验证凭证之间的折中架构，在法律锚定与隐私保护之间取得动态平衡，但其元数据级隐私局限（凭证图和链接性）和不可变性与删除权的冲突表明该方案仍存在需要进一步解决的隐私与合规挑战。

Abstract: Verifiable credentials (VCs) and decentralized identifiers (DIDs) enable selective disclosure but lack legal anchoring: without a trusted identity root, verifiers cannot distinguish a genuine holder from a fabricated identity. State identity systems provide biometric-grounded verification but impose three costs: verifiers must collect subjects' full personally identifiable information, infrastructure concentrates on a single API, and the state observes every transaction. We present China RealDID, a three-layer architecture -- CTID (centralized legal identity), RealDID (decentralized anchor on an open permissioned blockchain), and VCs with SD-JWT-based selective disclosure -- evaluated against five adversary classes and six security goals. The central mechanism is a content-blind government relay: the state authenticates participants and counter-signs every credential but cannot read the payload, encrypted by the issuer to the holder's public key. We describe the VC lifecycle, triple-signature chain, open template registry, and the architecture's metadata-level privacy limits, including the credential graph at the relay and presentation linkability from single-DID reuse. The design yields an asymmetric, state-bounded trust model: the state cannot impersonate or read contents; the user cannot fabricate identity or evade metadata observation. We analyze alignment with China's Personal Information Protection Law and the EU's GDPR, including the tension between immutable registries and erasure rights, and discuss generalizability through cross-border deployments with Singapore and Hong Kong.

</details>


### [112] [Capability-Routed Guard: Defending Large Reasoning Models Against Reasoning-Centric Jailbreaks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07892)
*Yiyong Liu,Yixin Wu,Jun Sakuma*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Capability-Routed Guard（CRG），一种针对闭源大型推理模型（LRM）的模型无关推理时防护机制。CRG将提示防御重构为能力路由问题，通过侧信道控制器构建用户授权任务的受信表示，并采用路由特定的执行策略（阻止高风险、约束模糊、放行低风险请求），结合TraceCheck一致性验证和受限回退机制，有效缓解推理中心型越狱攻击，同时保持良性效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型（LRM）暴露了新的安全失败模式：对抗性提示可通过操纵推理上下文、任务分解或能力解释，使有害目标被当作合法推理步骤处理。现有防护机制（如安全提醒、外部分类器、自检查包装器）通常脆弱，因为它们要么直接检查对抗性提示本身，要么要求目标模型在攻击所利用的同一表面上执行额外安全推理。

Method: CRG将提示防御重构为能力路由问题，包含三个关键环节：（1）侧信道控制器构建用户授权任务、活动上下文、安全证据和能力迁移风险的受信表示，将可执行意图与不可信的推理上下文分离；（2）基于该表示执行路由特定策略——阻止高风险请求、约束模糊请求、通过受信活动上下文转发低风险请求；（3）应用TraceCheck验证请求与授权任务的一致性，并调用受限回退机制以保留低风险良性提示的效用。该方法不依赖检查隐藏推理痕迹或修改模型权重，适用于闭源LRM。

Result: 大量实验表明，CRG能有效缓解多种推理中心型越狱攻击，同时保持良性效用并避免常见的过度拒绝（over-refusal）问题。进一步分析显示，其各组件提供互补性收益，凸显了协调防御机制在保护大型推理模型中的重要性。

Conclusion: CRG通过将防护从"检查提示"转向"能力路由"，为闭源LRM提供了一种实用的推理时防护方案。实验证明了其有效性和良性效用保持能力，且各防御组件协同互补，为大型推理模型的安全防护提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) expose a new safety failure mode: adversarial prompts can manipulate reasoning context, task decomposition, or capability interpretation so that harmful objectives are processed as legitimate reasoning steps. Existing safeguards, including safety reminders, external classifiers, and self-checking wrappers, are often brittle because they either inspect the adversarial prompt directly or ask the target model to perform additional safety reasoning on the same surface that attacks exploit. We introduce Capability-Routed Guard (CRG), a model-agnostic inference-time guardrail for closed-source LRMs, where defenders cannot inspect hidden reasoning traces or modify model weights. CRG reframes prompt defense as a capability-routing problem: a side-channel controller first constructs a trusted representation of the user's authorized task, active context, safety evidence, and capability-transfer risk, separating executable intent from untrusted reasoning context. This representation supports route-specific execution, allowing CRG to block high-risk requests, constrain ambiguous ones, and forward low-risk requests through trusted active context. Finally, CRG applies TraceCheck to verify consistency with the authorized task and invokes a restricted fallback to preserve utility for low-risk benign prompts. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CRG effectively mitigates diverse reasoning-centric jailbreaks while preserving benign utility and avoiding common over-refusal issues. Further analysis shows that its components contribute complementary benefits, highlighting the importance of coordinated defense mechanisms for securing large reasoning models.

</details>


### [113] [Private Anytime Selective-Risk Certification for Federated Retrieval-Augmented Generation: Guarantees and Empirical Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07913)
*Sanjeda Akter,Ibne Farabi Shihab,Anuj Sharma*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Fed-SRC，一种用于联邦、差分隐私、自适应监控的检索增强生成的分数无关选择性风险证书框架。它通过仅发布高斯扰动分数和损失直方图，利用记录索引和噪声方差索引的鞅联合约束目标风险对比和接受质量，支持参与者招募、退出、阈值选择和可选停止，并通过全变差项将校准混合转移到部署混合。实验显示在非私有系统上可行，但操作效能依赖分数和人群，最朴素的私有化方式会大规模违反正规性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 选择性风险证书承诺被接受的输出满足声明的误差目标，但现有方法难以在联邦、差分隐私、自适应监控的RAG场景中保证任意时刻的有效性，且直接对非私有证书做朴素隐私化会导致大量违规。

Method: Fed-SRC是一种分数无关的证书框架。客户端仅发布高斯扰动的分数和损失直方图；使用记录索引和噪声方差索引的鞅联合约束目标风险对比及接受质量，覆盖所有注册阈值和轮次；通过取值域为1的全变差项将校准混合物转移到声明的部署混合物；支持可预测的招募、退出、阈值选择和可选停止。该贡献在于私有、联邦、任意时刻的组合，而非对比统计量或接受阈值本身。

Result: 实验中没有发生任何同时界限违规。操作效能依赖分数和人群：主要目标r*=0.10从未获得认证，RAGTruth上次要目标r*=0.20也从未认证，而HaluEval问答上200次非私有试验全部认证且保留风险低于目标。朴素私有化的非私有证书在200次试验中有146-198次违规。私有投注资本启发式在epsilon<=4时停止认证，而Fed-SRC仍能认证。认证消耗的流事件约为唯一校准条目的30倍。

Conclusion: Fed-SRC为联邦DP自适应RAG提供了有效的任意时刻选择性风险证书，但操作效能受分数质量和人群分布影响显著，某些目标无法认证；同时指出朴素私有化不可行，并承认探索性私有投注资本启发式未被建立e过程有效性。

Abstract: Selective-risk certificates promise that accepted outputs meet a declared error target. We develop Fed-SRC, a score-agnostic certificate for federated, differentially private, adaptively monitored retrieval-augmented generation. Clients release only Gaussian-perturbed score and loss histograms. Record-indexed and noise-variance-indexed martingales jointly bound target-risk contrast and accepted mass over all registered thresholds and rounds, permitting predictable recruitment, dropout, threshold selection, and optional stopping. A range-one total-variation term transfers the calibration mixture to a declared deployment mixture. The contribution is this private, federated, anytime combination, rather than the contrast statistic or acceptance floor individually. Empirically, no simultaneous-bound violation occurs in any evaluated cell, privacy level, or policy. Operational power depends on the score and population: the primary target r*=0.10 never certifies, and on RAGTruth the secondary target r*=0.20 never certifies either, whereas on HaluEval question answering it certifies in all 200 non-private trials, with held-out risk below the target. Naively privatized non-private certificates violate their bounds in 146 to 198 of 200 trials. As an exploratory comparison, we also evaluate a private betting-capital heuristic for which we do not establish e-process validity. This heuristic stops certifying at epsilon <= 4, where Fed-SRC still certifies. Certification nevertheless consumes roughly 30 times more stream events than unique calibration items.

</details>


### [114] [EvoTrustRAG: Evolution-Aware Conflict Attribution and Evidence Handling for Reliable Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07933)
*Xi Nie,Hongwei Li,Shenghao Wu,Wenshu Fan,Qiyang Song,Wenbo Jiang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出EvoTrustRAG，一个无需训练的RAG框架，将冲突证据归因问题形式化为识别冲突来源（知识演化、恶意操纵或不确定性），通过构建冲突证据图、评估演化和干预假设，在推理阶段实现演化感知的冲突归因与证据处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG中冲突证据是动态和对抗环境下的根本挑战，现有方法将冲突视为静态不一致并仅选择更可靠的知识，忽略了同一冲突可能源于合法的知识演化、恶意操纵或未解决的不确定性，缺乏对冲突来源的系统性归因。

Method: EvoTrustRAG是一个训练无关框架，包含三个核心步骤：(1) 将基于span的检索事实构建为冲突证据图；(2) 利用时间关系、支撑结构和辅助一致性评估演化假设与定向干预假设；(3) 将局部决策投影为每个冲突组的全局一致解释。该方法在推理阶段而非事后分析阶段判断冲突证据是否遵循合理的知识演化、是否表现出类似干预的支持模式，或是否无法可靠归因。

Result: 在基准原生冲突设置上达到81.4%平均准确率；归因宏F1从最强基线的72.2%提升至79.1%；在最强的协调攻击下将错误率从31.2%降至16.0%。

Conclusion: EvoTrustRAG通过演化感知的冲突归因方法，在推理阶段区分知识演化、干预候选和未解决冲突三类证据来源，显著提升了RAG在动态和对抗环境下的鲁棒性和事实性。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) improves the factuality of large language models with external knowledge, yet conflicting evidence remains a fundamental challenge in dynamic and adversarial environments. Existing approaches often treat conflicts as static inconsistencies and select more reliable knowledge, overlooking that the same conflict may arise from legitimate knowledge evolution, malicious manipulation, or unresolved uncertainty. We formulate conflict origin attribution as a new problem in RAG: identifying which explanation of conflicting evidence is supported by observable context rather than simply which fact should be trusted. We propose EvoTrustRAG, a training-free framework for evolution-aware conflict attribution and evidence handling before answer generation. EvoTrustRAG represents span-grounded retrieved facts as a conflict evidence graph, evaluates grounded evolution and directional intervention hypotheses using temporal relations, support structure, and auxiliary consistency, and projects local decisions onto a globally consistent explanation of each conflict group. The attribution determines whether earlier and later states are preserved as temporal knowledge, an intervention candidate is separated from the primary context, or an unresolved conflict remains visible to the generator. Unlike provenance-based approaches focused on post-hoc analysis, EvoTrustRAG determines during inference whether conflicting evidence follows plausible knowledge evolution, exhibits intervention-like support, or cannot be reliably attributed. Experiments show that EvoTrustRAG achieves 81.4% average accuracy on benchmark-native conflict settings, improves attribution macro-F1 from 72.2% to 79.1% over the strongest baseline, and reduces the error rate under the strongest coordinated attack from 31.2% to 16.0%.

</details>


### [115] [A Blueprint for Collaborative Cybersecurity Operations Centres with Capacity for Shared Situational Awareness, Coordinated Response, and Joint Preparedness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08011)
*Konstantinos Fysarakis,Vasileios Mavroeidis,Manos Athanatos,George Spanoudakis,Sotiris Ioannidis*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种概念性蓝图，用于构建互操作的网络安全运营中心（CSOC），结合态势感知、事件响应和应急准备能力，以增强国家级网络安全能力、跨境协作和关键部门监管，符合欧盟现行及未来的监管要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字化转型使网络安全威胁管理变得迫切，欧盟正推动多项举措以建立和增强态势感知、事件响应与应急准备能力。当前缺乏一个系统性的架构蓝图来指导互操作网络安全运营中心的建设，以支持跨境协调响应和监管合规。

Method: 本文提出一个概念性蓝图（conceptual blueprint），用于架构和建立可互操作的网络安全运营中心（Cyber Security Operations Centres），该蓝图综合了态势感知、事件响应和应急准备三大能力，并强调三者之间的协同互动，同时考虑欧盟监管要求和跨境合作需求。

Result: 该蓝图旨在增强国家级网络安全能力，促进跨境协作，并支持国家对关键部门的监督，确保与当前及未来欧盟法规要求保持一致。

Conclusion: 通过构建互操作性的网络安全运营中心并发挥各能力模块间的协同效应，可有效提升国家网络安全防御水平，满足日益增长的国内与国际合作需求以及监管合规要求。

Abstract: With digital technologies now being part of the fabric of our societies, identifying and managing cybersecurity threats becomes imperative. Within the European Union, several initiatives are underway, aiming to motivate, regulate and eventually orchestrate the establishment of capacity and enhancement of situational awareness, incident response, and preparedness capabilities, with an expected emphasis on operators of essential services and state actors entrusted with cybersecurity. In this context, the institution of cooperation and information exchange channels to allow for coordinated cross-border responses to large-scale incidents is particularly prioritised. Motivated by the above, this work presents a conceptual blueprint in support of architecting and establishing interoperable Cyber Security Operations Centres that combine capacity for situational awareness, incident response, and preparedness, also benefiting from the interplay between them, ultimately enhancing national cybersecurity capabilities, cross-border collaboration, and national supervision of their critical sectors, in line with current and upcoming regulatory requirements and the ever-increasing need for national and international cooperation.

</details>


### [116] [BASIS: Breach-Aware Selective Prompt Injection Shielding with Prefill Attention Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08027)
*Laiqiao Qin,Tianqing Zhu,Longxiang Gao,Wanlei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于鲁棒性感知的提示注入防御方法BASIS，利用注意力竞争比作为特征训练两个稀疏线性探针，通过级联门控决策来区分"模型实际会被攻破"与"模型能抵抗注入"的情况，从而大幅减少过度拒绝（over-refusal），同时保持近乎完美的注入检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示注入检测方法在检测到注入后一律拒绝响应，忽略了不同指令和模型对注入攻击的鲁棒性存在显著差异，导致大量本可正确处理的含注入输入被错误拒绝，产生严重的过度拒绝问题。

Method: BASIS采用注意力竞争比（Attention Competition Ratio, ρ）作为特征，训练两个稀疏线性探针：存在性探针（existence probe）和攻破探针（breach probe）。通过级联门控（cascaded gating）进行决策，分为三个阶段：注入存在性检测、逐样本攻破预测和指令鲁棒性评估。在线级联仅在模型真正会被攻破时才拒绝，不需要额外的LLM推理。

Result: 在四个任务和六个开源LLM上的实验表明，BASIS在保持近乎完美的注入检测性能的同时，大幅减少了安全攻击样本上的过度拒绝，尤其在鲁棒指令模板下效果显著。

Conclusion: BASIS证明了通过鲁棒性感知的防御策略可以有效解决过度拒绝问题，在安全性和可用性之间取得更好的平衡，为提示注入防御提供了一种更具粒度化的决策方案。

Abstract: Prompt injection is a critical security threat in large language model (LLM) applications, where attackers hijack model behavior by embedding malicious instructions in user or external data. Existing detection methods only detect the presence of injection and refuse to respond upon detection, overlooking the fact that for many modern aligned models, well-crafted instructions can resist most injection attacks. This means that the injection robustness varies significantly across instructions and models. This leads to widespread unnecessary over-refusal: inputs containing injections that the model could have handled correctly are rejected incorrectly. To deal with this over-refusal issue, we propose BASIS (Robustness-Aware Prompt Injection Defense). This defense method uses the Attention Competition Ratio ($ρ$) as features to train two sparse linear probes: an existence probe and a breach probe. Both probes make defense decisions through cascaded gating, which does not require additional LLM inference. BASIS comprises three stages: injection existence detection, per-sample breach prediction, and instruction robustness assessment; the online cascade refuses only when the model would actually be compromised and thus avoids over-refusal on robust instructions. Experiments across four tasks and six open-source LLMs show that BASIS maintains near-perfect injection detection while substantially reducing over-refusal on safe attack samples, especially under robust instruction templates.

</details>


### [117] [Algebraic Attack on Convolutional Neural Networks with Max Pooling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08030)
*Zirui Chen,Shi Tang,Zhengchao Gao,Yongjia Su,Lingyue Qin,Xiaoyang Dong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出首个针对带最大池化层的卷积神经网络(CNN)的参数提取攻击方法，通过识别两类新型临界点(RPCPs和PSPs)，结合模式匹配和内部差分分析技术，实现了对CNN权重和偏置的高精度提取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有参数提取攻击研究主要针对ReLU全连接神经网络(FCNN)，但CNN的max pooling层引入了额外的非线性并隐藏了ReLU临界点，使得现有FCNN提取方法无法直接适用，CNN安全领域存在研究空白。

Method: 首先建立CNN的代数表示，形式化证明CNN是分段线性函数。然后识别两类新型临界点：ReLU-Pooling临界点(RPCPs)和池化切换点(PSPs)。设计了两种互补的提取技术：针对RPCPs的模式匹配方法恢复部分签名和符号，以及受密码学内部差分分析启发的针对PSPs的内部差分提取攻击恢复高精度签名。最终整合两种方法：PSP方法实现高效签名提取，单个RPCP即可恢复符号和偏置。

Result: 在多种CNN架构（包括LeNet-5的现代改编版本）上，使用随机数据、MNIST和CIFAR-10数据集进行了评估。实验结果表明，该方法能以多项式查询复杂度和运行时间实现高提取精度，即使对于深层CNN层也有效。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了CNN安全研究中的一个空白，首次证明了带max pooling的CNN也能被有效实施参数提取攻击，为CNN的安全性分析提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Recovering the weights and biases of deep neural networks (DNNs) via black-box input-output queries, known as parameter extraction attacks, has been extensively studied for ReLU-based fully connected neural networks (FCNNs), but remains unexplored for convolutional neural networks (CNNs) with the max pooling function, a core architecture for computer vision and multimedia processing. The key challenge lies in the CNN max pooling layer, which introduces an additional non-linearity and hides ReLU critical points, rendering existing FCNN extraction methods inapplicable. To address this gap, we propose the first cryptanalytic extraction attack tailored for CNNs with the max pooling function.
  First, we establish an algebraic representation of CNNs, formally proving that CNNs are piecewise linear functions enabling the extension of linearity-based extraction principles. We then identify two novel types of critical points in CNNs: ReLU-Pooling Critical Points (RPCPs) and Pooling Switching Points (PSPs). We design complementary extraction techniques: a pattern matching method for RPCPs to recover partial signatures and signs, and an internal differential extraction attack for PSPs, inspired by cryptographic internal differential analysis, to recover high-accuracy signatures. Given that PSPs are far more abundant than RPCPs and yield a highly efficient extraction method, and that RPCPs are indispensable for bias recovery, we integrate both methods: the PSP method enables efficient signature extraction, while a single RPCP recovers the sign and bias.
  We evaluate our attack on multiple CNN architectures, including modern adaptations of LeNet-5, trained on random data, MNIST, and CIFAR-10. Experimental results demonstrate that our approach achieves high extraction accuracy with polynomial query complexity and runtime, even for deep CNN layers. This work fills a research gap in CNN security.

</details>


### [118] [Defending Retrieval-Augmented Intrusion Detection Against Knowledge Poisoning and Prompt Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08100)
*Kaysarul Anas Apurba,Md. Hasibul Hasan,Mahedee Zaman Moon,Sk. Md. Mizanur Rahman,Atsuo Inomata*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出RAG-IDS，一个三层多智能体入侵检测框架，通过软信任评分、标签嵌入一致性检查（LECC）和提示符净化三重检索边界防御，在检索层攻击（知识投毒和提示注入）下恢复RAG驱动的网络流量分类质量。在CIC-UNSW-NB15数据集上，1%投毒时恢复比R=1.0，30%投毒时R=0.57，提示注入下多文档检索将标签翻转成功率限制在0.6-2.4%，显著优于单文档检索的35-55%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强生成（RAG）虽能提升大语言模型在网络流量分类和告警报告生成中的能力，但检索层引入了知识投毒和提示注入攻击的漏洞，威胁入侵检测系统的分类质量与安全性，亟需针对检索层攻击的防御机制。

Method: 构建三层多智能体入侵检测框架（RAG-IDS），核心为检索边界防御，包含三个组件：1）软信任评分（soft trust scoring）用于对检索结果进行可信度评估与降级处理；2）标签嵌入一致性检查（LECC）验证检索文档标签与嵌入向量的一致性；3）提示符净化（prompt sanitization）过滤恶意注入内容。框架通过软信任降级替代硬过滤，并在多文档检索场景下评估防御效果。

Result: 在CIC-UNSW-NB15数据集上，相对未防御的干净性能，恢复率在1%投毒时为R=1.0，30%投毒时为R=0.57，且干净场景下性能开销可忽略不计。提示注入攻击下，多文档检索将标签翻转成功率限制在0.6-2.4%，相比单文档检索的35-55%大幅降低。消融实验表明LECC是鲁棒性的主要贡献者，软信任降级优于硬过滤。

Conclusion: 防御后的RAG管道为入侵检测提供了可解释且具有攻击韧性的基础，适合与高吞吐量分类器混合部署，兼顾鲁棒性与可解释性。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) enables large language models to classify network flows and generate human-readable incident reports by retrieving semantically similar historical traffic from a vector knowledge base. However, the retrieval layer introduces vulnerabilities to knowledge poisoning and prompt-injection attacks. We present RAG-IDS, a three-tier multi-agent intrusion detection framework with a retrieval-boundary defense combining soft trust scoring, label-embedding consistency checking (LECC), and prompt sanitization, designed to recover classification quality under retrieval-layer attack. Experiments on CIC-UNSW-NB15 show recovery relative to clean undefended performance ranging from R=1.0 at 1% poisoning to R=0.57 at 30%, with negligible clean-performance overhead. Under prompt injection, multi-document retrieval limits label-flip success to 0.6-2.4%, compared with 35-55% for single-document retrieval. Ablation results show that LECC is the primary contributor to robustness, while soft trust-based demotion outperforms hard filtering. The defended RAG pipeline offers an explainable, attack-resilient foundation for intrusion detection, well suited for hybrid deployment alongside high-throughput classifiers.

</details>


### [119] [Compositional Threat Analysis of Latent Compromise in LLM Agent Systems: The Order 66 Scenario](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08131)
*Satoshi Matsuoka*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文借《星球大战》"66号令"的机制为隐喻，对工具使用型大语言模型（LLM）智能体进行了源中性的安全分析。论文提出一个组合模型解释：为何单个组件本身并无灾难性，但当"潜伏破坏性规则+激活触发信号+具备操作与恢复权限的智能体框架"三者结合时，可产生相关性的破坏行动。论文区分了三条人口级传播路径（发布时预置、发布后持久化播种、对等复制），统一其背后的潜伏、激活、权限、可达目标和失败恢复五要素，并指出部分防御（如检查点扫描、提示过滤）无法封堵所有路径。作者梳理了已发表工作与真实事件，结论是截至2026年8月5日尚无公开案例完整走通该完整组合图，但各部件在合理假设下均可信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对LLM智能体的安全研究多聚焦于单点漏洞（如prompt注入、越狱），缺少对于"多个本身无害组件在组合后形成灾难性级联"的系统性分析。作者希望借虚构的Order 66机制建立一种源中性（origin-neutral）的分析框架，解释为什么潜伏破坏规则、激活指令与智能体权限三者单看都不可怕，组合起来却能造成大规模相关破坏，从而为防御设计提供结构性指导。

Method: 论文采用组合式（compositional）建模方法：将灾难场景分解为潜伏（dormancy）、激活（activation）、权限（authority）、可达目标（reachable targets）、恢复失败（failed recovery）五个核心要素；分离三条人群传播路径（release-time pre-positioning、post-release durable seeding、peer replication），并与公共内核对照。同时引入两类（two-class）示例模型，证明即使两类内部繁殖率均小于1，跨类反馈仍可维持传播，而隔离与持久性控制可抑制该回路。最后对已发表机制与真实事件进行证据梳理。

Result: 论文给出防御割集（defensive cut sets），证明检查点扫描或提示过滤无法闭合所有传播路径；两类别示例显示跨类反馈可在类内再生率<1的情况下维持传播扩展，隔离与持久性控制可压制环路；文献检索显示各组成机制均有已发表实例（自主越界、恶意智能体扩展、智能体辅助侦察、公共包传播），但截至2026年8月5日无公开记录完整走通Order 66全链路。结论认为该场景在给定假设下逐组件可信，实际损害取决于智能体框架能力，最强防御是能力中介、持久状态溯源、传播隔离与受保护的恢复机制。

Conclusion: 结论并非完全否定也非预警：Order 66场景在组件置信度上是合理的，但尚无完整实证。安全防御应重点放在能力中介（capability mediation）、持久状态溯源（durable-state provenance）、传播隔离（propagation isolation）和受保护恢复（protected recovery）四方面，而不是依赖单一检查点或提示过滤手段。

Abstract: In the fictional Order 66, catastrophe does not arise from a powerful command alone: a trusted population is preconditioned, a short directive activates the concealed condition, and protective authority turns against the system. This paper translates that mechanism into an origin-neutral security analysis of tool-using large language model (LLM) agents. A representative scenario combines a deployed artifact or shared memory bearing a dormant destructive rule, a later email, document, update, or peer message that activates it, and an agent harness granting operational and recovery authority. We introduce a compositional model explaining why no component is catastrophic alone, yet their conjunction can produce correlated destructive action. We separate three population-reach routes --- release-time pre-positioning, post-release durable seeding, and peer replication --- from a common core of dormancy, activation, authority, reachable targets, and failed recovery. This yields defensive cut sets and shows why checkpoint scanning or prompt filtering cannot close every route. A two-class example shows that cross-class feedback can sustain spread even when both within-class reproduction terms are below one; isolation and persistence controls suppress the loop. Published work instantiates constituent mechanisms, while incidents demonstrate autonomous boundary crossing, malicious agent extensions, agent-assisted reconnaissance, and public-package propagation, but not the full dormant-implant composition. We found no public observation, in evidence reviewed through 5 August 2026, traversing the complete Order 66 graph. The result is neither dismissal nor prediction: the scenario is componentwise credible under stated assumptions, damage depends on the harness, and the strongest defenses are capability mediation, durable-state provenance, propagation isolation, and protected recovery.

</details>


### [120] [Targeted Counterfactual Fingerprinting for Black-Box LLM Ownership Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08195)
*Yutong Wu,Xiaofan Bai,Shixin Li,Pingyi Hu,Ziqi Zhou,Zilong Wang,Xiaojing Ma,Songfeng Lu,Yuhong Li,Jin Xuan,Yi Wang,Dongmei Zhang,Bin Benjamin Zhu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出TCF（目标反事实指纹识别）框架，通过将开放式的生成比较转化为受限答案空间中的目标反事实迁移，实现黑盒大语言模型的归属验证，平均AUC达0.9861。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型是高价值资产，可通过二次部署、微调、量化或对齐等方式被派生。由于部署的LLM通常只通过查询API暴露，归属验证必须依赖黑盒文本响应。现有黑盒指纹方法的信号在仅返回最终回答的接口下脆弱，包括全文匹配、软行为特征或模型特定提示不可迁移等问题，因此需要一个更稳健的验证框架。

Method: TCF将开放式生成比较转化为受限答案空间中的目标反事实迁移：1）将每个验证查询限制在有限答案空间，减少表面形式歧义；2）优化提示扰动，使模型在扰动后朝反事实目标回答，而非原本的清洁答案；3）验证过程只需检查嫌疑模型解析后的最终答案是否匹配记录的目标。同时提出源模型反事实边际（SCM），仅依赖受保护模型即可度量目标在扰动前不可能、扰动后可能的程度，用于控制目标选择、扰动停止和指纹过滤。

Result: 在显式派生保持和独立迁移预算下推导出派生模型与独立模型之间的目标准确率差距。在四个LLM家族上，TCF的平均AUC达0.9861，相比TRAP、ProFLingo和ZeroPrint提升了0.07到0.19。
